The Maker’s Bench artwork catalog

The sports, holiday, and seasonal collections include original Maker’s Bench drawings and selections from the libraries credited below. A curated selection keeps its original artwork ID and export attribution.

The Dachshund and Rolled diploma silhouettes are original Maker’s Bench illustrations.

Artwork with a catalog ID starting with “fa-” comes from Font Awesome Free 6.7.2
by Fonticons, Inc., copyright 2024. The SVG icons are licensed under Creative
Commons Attribution 4.0 International (CC BY 4.0). The studio converts their
outlines to absolute cubic paths, scales them to fit, and applies your colors.
The catalog includes descriptive labels and search terms from the same release.

Source: https://github.com/FortAwesome/Font-Awesome/tree/6.7.2
License: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/
Version and notices: https://fontawesome.com/license/free

“Icon adapted from Font Awesome Free 6.7.2 by Fonticons, Inc.
(https://fontawesome.com), licensed under CC BY 4.0
(https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/).”

Awesome Free files already contain embedded comments with sufficient
attribution, so you shouldn't need to do anything additional when using these

Licensed under Creative Commons Attribution 3.0 Unported (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/). The studio removes the tile background, converts the foreground to absolute, rounded cubic contours, then sizes and colors it for each project. Source pages and creators follow.

Maker’s Bench campus, transportation, and illustration collections

Official mark usage guide: https://www.uh.edu/brand/brand-identity/master-brand-logos-and-marks/interlocking-uh/

Additional library artwork

The studio converts each source shape to compact, filled outlines. It resolves overlapping contours and converts arcs to cubic curves for fabrication. Coloring and sizing are applied by the project. Icons in this expansion use the following source collections. The per-image entries below give the original stable icon name and collection.
